Do not edit any other file.\n\npython3.12 - <<'PY'\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nOLD_NEXT = 'Start the two queued KX-06 packets: KX-06-01 (signals feed on Home, priority 8) then KX-06-02 (plain-English deadline/phone/what-changed cards).
